# 8. Migration properties

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.config;

import java.time.LocalTime;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;

@ConfigurationProperties(prefix = "migration")
public record MigrationProperties(
    boolean enabled,
    String timezone,
    long activeRunId,
    StatusLoad statusLoad,
    Execution execution,
    Retry retry,
    Recovery recovery
) {
    public record StatusLoad(
        boolean enabled,
        LocalTime start,
        LocalTime end,
        long schedulerDelayMs,
        int pageSize
    ) {}

    public record Execution(
        boolean enabled,
        LocalTime start,
        LocalTime stopClaim,
        LocalTime hardStop,
        long schedulerDelayMs,
        int candidateReadSize,
        int claimSize
    ) {}

    public record Retry(
        LocalTime start,
        LocalTime end,
        long schedulerDelayMs,
        int claimSize,
        int maximumRetries
    ) {}

    public record Recovery(
        long schedulerDelayMs,
        int staleClaimMinutes
    ) {}
}
```

Register it:

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.config;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.ZoneId;

import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.EnableConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

@Configuration
@EnableConfigurationProperties(MigrationProperties.class)
public class SchedulerConfiguration {

    @Bean
    public Clock migrationClock(
            MigrationProperties properties) {

        return Clock.system(
            ZoneId.of(properties.timezone())
        );
    }
}
```

# 9. Worker identity

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.config;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

@Component
public class WorkerIdentity {

    private final String workerId;

    public WorkerIdentity(
            @Value("${MIGRATION_DC:LOCAL}")
            String dataCentre,

            @Value("${CF_INSTANCE_INDEX:0}")
            String instanceIndex,

            @Value("${CF_INSTANCE_GUID:LOCAL}")
            String instanceGuid) {

        this.workerId = String.format(
            "%s-%s-%s",
            dataCentre,
            instanceIndex,
            instanceGuid
        );
    }

    public String getWorkerId() {
        return workerId;
    }
}
```

# 10. Time-window service

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.service;

import com.company.personalisationmigration.config.MigrationProperties;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.LocalTime;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class MigrationWindowService {

    private final Clock migrationClock;
    private final MigrationProperties properties;

    public boolean canLoadExistingStatuses() {
        return properties.enabled()
            && properties.statusLoad().enabled()
            && isBetween(
                properties.statusLoad().start(),
                properties.statusLoad().end()
            );
    }

    public boolean canClaimMigrationWork() {
        return properties.enabled()
            && properties.execution().enabled()
            && isBetween(
                properties.execution().start(),
                properties.execution().stopClaim()
            );
    }

    public boolean canFinishMigrationWork() {
        return properties.enabled()
            && isBetween(
                properties.execution().start(),
                properties.execution().hardStop()
            );
    }

    public boolean canRetry() {
        return properties.enabled()
            && isBetween(
                properties.retry().start(),
                properties.retry().end()
            );
    }

    private boolean isBetween(
            LocalTime start,
            LocalTime end) {

        LocalTime now = LocalTime.now(migrationClock);

        return !now.isBefore(start)
            && now.isBefore(end);
    }
}
```

# 13. DB2 batch-control repository

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.db2;

import com.company.personalisationmigration.model.MigrationBatch;
import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Repository
public class Db2BatchControlRepository {

    private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;

    public Db2BatchControlRepository(
            @Qualifier("db2JdbcTemplate")
            JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate) {

        this.jdbcTemplate = jdbcTemplate;
    }

    @Transactional(
        transactionManager = "db2TransactionManager"
    )
    public Optional<MigrationBatch> claimNextBatch(
            long runId,
            String phase,
            String workerId) {

        for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 10; attempt++) {

            List<MigrationBatch> candidates =
                jdbcTemplate.query(
                    """
                    SELECT
                        RUN_ID,
                        PHASE,
                        BATCH_ID,
                        START_CUSTOMER_ID,
                        END_CUSTOMER_ID,
                        LAST_PROCESSED_ID,
                        VERSION_NO,
                        CLAIMED_BY,
                        CLAIMED_AT
                    FROM MIGRATION_BATCH_CONTROL
                    WHERE RUN_ID = ?
                      AND PHASE = ?
                      AND BATCH_STATUS = 'PENDING'
                    ORDER BY BATCH_ID
                    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
                    """,
                    (rs, rowNum) -> new MigrationBatch(
                        rs.getLong("RUN_ID"),
                        rs.getString("PHASE"),
                        rs.getLong("BATCH_ID"),
                        rs.getLong("START_CUSTOMER_ID"),
                        getNullableLong(rs, "END_CUSTOMER_ID"),
                        getNullableLong(rs, "LAST_PROCESSED_ID"),
                        rs.getLong("VERSION_NO"),
                        rs.getString("CLAIMED_BY"),
                        toInstant(rs.getTimestamp("CLAIMED_AT"))
                    ),
                    runId,
                    phase
                );

            if (candidates.isEmpty()) {
                return Optional.empty();
            }

            MigrationBatch candidate = candidates.get(0);

            int updated = jdbcTemplate.update(
                """
                UPDATE MIGRATION_BATCH_CONTROL
                SET BATCH_STATUS = 'CLAIMED',
                    CLAIMED_BY = ?,
                    CLAIMED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                    LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                    VERSION_NO = VERSION_NO + 1
                WHERE RUN_ID = ?
                  AND PHASE = ?
                  AND BATCH_ID = ?
                  AND BATCH_STATUS = 'PENDING'
                  AND VERSION_NO = ?
                """,
                workerId,
                runId,
                phase,
                candidate.batchId(),
                candidate.versionNo()
            );

            if (updated == 1) {
                return Optional.of(
                    new MigrationBatch(
                        candidate.runId(),
                        candidate.phase(),
                        candidate.batchId(),
                        candidate.startCustomerId(),
                        candidate.endCustomerId(),
                        candidate.lastProcessedId(),
                        candidate.versionNo() + 1,
                        workerId,
                        Instant.now()
                    )
                );
            }
        }

        return Optional.empty();
    }

    public void markInProgress(
            MigrationBatch batch,
            String workerId) {

        jdbcTemplate.update(
            """
            UPDATE MIGRATION_BATCH_CONTROL
            SET BATCH_STATUS = 'IN_PROGRESS',
                LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND PHASE = ?
              AND BATCH_ID = ?
              AND CLAIMED_BY = ?
              AND BATCH_STATUS = 'CLAIMED'
            """,
            batch.runId(),
            batch.phase(),
            batch.batchId(),
            workerId
        );
    }

    public void updateCheckpoint(
            MigrationBatch batch,
            String workerId,
            long lastCustomerId,
            long readCount,
            long insertedCount) {

        jdbcTemplate.update(
            """
            UPDATE MIGRATION_BATCH_CONTROL
            SET LAST_PROCESSED_ID = ?,
                READ_COUNT = ?,
                INSERTED_COUNT = ?,
                LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND PHASE = ?
              AND BATCH_ID = ?
              AND CLAIMED_BY = ?
              AND BATCH_STATUS = 'IN_PROGRESS'
            """,
            lastCustomerId,
            readCount,
            insertedCount,
            batch.runId(),
            batch.phase(),
            batch.batchId(),
            workerId
        );
    }

    public void markCompleted(
            MigrationBatch batch,
            String workerId,
            long readCount,
            long insertedCount) {

        jdbcTemplate.update(
            """
            UPDATE MIGRATION_BATCH_CONTROL
            SET BATCH_STATUS = 'COMPLETED',
                READ_COUNT = ?,
                INSERTED_COUNT = ?,
                COMPLETED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND PHASE = ?
              AND BATCH_ID = ?
              AND CLAIMED_BY = ?
            """,
            readCount,
            insertedCount,
            batch.runId(),
            batch.phase(),
            batch.batchId(),
            workerId
        );
    }

    public void markFailed(
            MigrationBatch batch,
            String workerId,
            String errorMessage) {

        jdbcTemplate.update(
            """
            UPDATE MIGRATION_BATCH_CONTROL
            SET BATCH_STATUS = 'FAILED',
                RETRY_COUNT = RETRY_COUNT + 1,
                ERROR_MESSAGE = ?,
                LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND PHASE = ?
              AND BATCH_ID = ?
              AND CLAIMED_BY = ?
            """,
            truncate(errorMessage, 1900),
            batch.runId(),
            batch.phase(),
            batch.batchId(),
            workerId
        );
    }

    private static Long getNullableLong(
            java.sql.ResultSet rs,
            String column) throws java.sql.SQLException {

        long value = rs.getLong(column);
        return rs.wasNull() ? null : value;
    }

    private static Instant toInstant(Timestamp value) {
        return value == null ? null : value.toInstant();
    }

    private static String truncate(
            String value,
            int maximumLength) {

        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }

        return value.substring(
            0,
            Math.min(value.length(), maximumLength)
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 14. Read existing migrated customer IDs from MariaDB

Replace `TARGET_PERSONALISATION` with your actual MariaDB table.

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.mariadb;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

@Repository
public class MariaDbExistingCustomerRepository {

    private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;

    public MariaDbExistingCustomerRepository(
            @Qualifier("mariaDbJdbcTemplate")
            JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate) {

        this.jdbcTemplate = jdbcTemplate;
    }

    public List<Long> findCustomerIds(
            long lastCustomerId,
            Long endCustomerId,
            int limit) {

        if (endCustomerId == null) {
            return jdbcTemplate.queryForList(
                """
                SELECT DISTINCT CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
                FROM TARGET_PERSONALISATION
                WHERE CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID > ?
                ORDER BY CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
                LIMIT ?
                """,
                Long.class,
                lastCustomerId,
                limit
            );
        }

        return jdbcTemplate.queryForList(
            """
            SELECT DISTINCT CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
            FROM TARGET_PERSONALISATION
            WHERE CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID > ?
              AND CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID <= ?
            ORDER BY CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
            LIMIT ?
            """,
            Long.class,
            lastCustomerId,
            endCustomerId,
            limit
        );
    }

    public Set<Long> findExistingCustomers(
            List<Long> customerIds) {

        if (customerIds.isEmpty()) {
            return Set.of();
        }

        String placeholders = String.join(
            ",",
            java.util.Collections.nCopies(
                customerIds.size(),
                "?"
            )
        );

        String sql = """
            SELECT DISTINCT CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
            FROM TARGET_PERSONALISATION
            WHERE CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID IN (%s)
            """.formatted(placeholders);

        List<Long> result = jdbcTemplate.queryForList(
            sql,
            Long.class,
            customerIds.toArray()
        );

        return result.stream().collect(Collectors.toSet());
    }
}
```

For large IDs lists, keep each check batch at approximately 100–250 customers.

---

# 15. DB2 migration-status repository

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.db2;

import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.util.List;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
import org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.BatchPreparedStatementSetter;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Repository
public class Db2MigrationStatusRepository {

    private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;

    public Db2MigrationStatusRepository(
            @Qualifier("db2JdbcTemplate")
            JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate) {

        this.jdbcTemplate = jdbcTemplate;
    }

    @Transactional(
        transactionManager = "db2TransactionManager"
    )
    public int insertAlreadyMigrated(
            long runId,
            List<Long> customerIds) {

        int inserted = 0;

        for (Long customerId : customerIds) {
            try {
                int result = jdbcTemplate.update(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS
                    (
                        RUN_ID,
                        CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
                        MIGRATION_STATUS,
                        COMPLETED_AT,
                        CREATED_AT,
                        UPDATED_AT
                    )
                    VALUES
                    (
                        ?,
                        ?,
                        'ALREADY_MIGRATED',
                        CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                        CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                        CURRENT TIMESTAMP
                    )
                    """,
                    runId,
                    customerId
                );

                inserted += result;

            } catch (DuplicateKeyException ignored) {
                // Safe replay. Never overwrite an existing status.
            }
        }

        return inserted;
    }

    public boolean claimCustomer(
            long runId,
            long customerId,
            String workerId) {

        try {
            int inserted = jdbcTemplate.update(
                """
                INSERT INTO CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS
                (
                    RUN_ID,
                    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
                    MIGRATION_STATUS,
                    CLAIMED_BY,
                    CLAIMED_AT,
                    LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT,
                    STARTED_AT,
                    CREATED_AT,
                    UPDATED_AT
                )
                VALUES
                (
                    ?,
                    ?,
                    'IN_PROGRESS',
                    ?,
                    CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                    CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                    CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                    CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                    CURRENT TIMESTAMP
                )
                """,
                runId,
                customerId,
                workerId
            );

            return inserted == 1;

        } catch (DuplicateKeyException ignored) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    public boolean claimRetry(
            long runId,
            long customerId,
            String workerId,
            int maximumRetries) {

        int updated = jdbcTemplate.update(
            """
            UPDATE CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS
            SET MIGRATION_STATUS = 'IN_PROGRESS',
                CLAIMED_BY = ?,
                CLAIMED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                STARTED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                UPDATED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID = ?
              AND MIGRATION_STATUS = 'RETRY_PENDING'
              AND RETRY_COUNT < ?
            """,
            workerId,
            runId,
            customerId,
            maximumRetries
        );

        return updated == 1;
    }

    public List<Long> findRetryCandidates(
            long runId,
            int maximumRetries,
            int limit) {

        return jdbcTemplate.queryForList(
            """
            SELECT CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
            FROM CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND MIGRATION_STATUS = 'RETRY_PENDING'
              AND RETRY_COUNT < ?
            ORDER BY UPDATED_AT,
                     CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
            FETCH FIRST ? ROWS ONLY
            """,
            Long.class,
            runId,
            maximumRetries,
            limit
        );
    }

    public void markAlreadyMigrated(
            long runId,
            long customerId,
            String workerId) {

        jdbcTemplate.update(
            """
            UPDATE CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS
            SET MIGRATION_STATUS = 'ALREADY_MIGRATED',
                COMPLETED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                UPDATED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                ERROR_CODE = NULL,
                ERROR_MESSAGE = NULL
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID = ?
              AND MIGRATION_STATUS = 'IN_PROGRESS'
              AND CLAIMED_BY = ?
            """,
            runId,
            customerId,
            workerId
        );
    }

    public void markMigrated(
            long runId,
            long customerId,
            String workerId,
            int sourceCount,
            int insertedCount,
            int updatedCount) {

        jdbcTemplate.update(
            """
            UPDATE CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS
            SET MIGRATION_STATUS = 'MIGRATED',
                COMPLETED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                UPDATED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                SOURCE_RECORD_COUNT = ?,
                INSERTED_RECORD_COUNT = ?,
                UPDATED_RECORD_COUNT = ?,
                ERROR_CODE = NULL,
                ERROR_MESSAGE = NULL
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID = ?
              AND MIGRATION_STATUS = 'IN_PROGRESS'
              AND CLAIMED_BY = ?
            """,
            sourceCount,
            insertedCount,
            updatedCount,
            runId,
            customerId,
            workerId
        );
    }

    public void markSourceNotFound(
            long runId,
            long customerId,
            String workerId) {

        jdbcTemplate.update(
            """
            UPDATE CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS
            SET MIGRATION_STATUS = 'SOURCE_NOT_FOUND',
                COMPLETED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                UPDATED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                ERROR_CODE = 'SOURCE_NOT_FOUND',
                ERROR_MESSAGE =
                    'No eligible source personalisation records found'
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID = ?
              AND MIGRATION_STATUS = 'IN_PROGRESS'
              AND CLAIMED_BY = ?
            """,
            runId,
            customerId,
            workerId
        );
    }

    public void markRetryPending(
            long runId,
            long customerId,
            String workerId,
            int maximumRetries,
            String errorCode,
            String errorMessage) {

        jdbcTemplate.update(
            """
            UPDATE CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS
            SET MIGRATION_STATUS =
                CASE
                    WHEN RETRY_COUNT + 1 < ?
                        THEN 'RETRY_PENDING'
                    ELSE 'FAILED'
                END,
                RETRY_COUNT = RETRY_COUNT + 1,
                CLAIMED_BY = NULL,
                CLAIMED_AT = NULL,
                LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT = NULL,
                COMPLETED_AT =
                CASE
                    WHEN RETRY_COUNT + 1 >= ?
                        THEN CURRENT TIMESTAMP
                    ELSE NULL
                END,
                UPDATED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                ERROR_CODE = ?,
                ERROR_MESSAGE = ?
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID = ?
              AND MIGRATION_STATUS = 'IN_PROGRESS'
              AND CLAIMED_BY = ?
            """,
            maximumRetries,
            maximumRetries,
            truncate(errorCode, 100),
            truncate(errorMessage, 1900),
            runId,
            customerId,
            workerId
        );
    }

    public void markFailed(
            long runId,
            long customerId,
            String workerId,
            String errorCode,
            String errorMessage) {

        jdbcTemplate.update(
            """
            UPDATE CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS
            SET MIGRATION_STATUS = 'FAILED',
                RETRY_COUNT = RETRY_COUNT + 1,
                COMPLETED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                UPDATED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                ERROR_CODE = ?,
                ERROR_MESSAGE = ?
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID = ?
              AND MIGRATION_STATUS = 'IN_PROGRESS'
              AND CLAIMED_BY = ?
            """,
            truncate(errorCode, 100),
            truncate(errorMessage, 1900),
            runId,
            customerId,
            workerId
        );
    }

    public int recoverStaleClaims(
            long runId,
            int maximumRetries,
            int staleMinutes) {

        String sql = """
            UPDATE CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS
            SET MIGRATION_STATUS =
                CASE
                    WHEN RETRY_COUNT + 1 < ?
                        THEN 'RETRY_PENDING'
                    ELSE 'FAILED'
                END,
                RETRY_COUNT = RETRY_COUNT + 1,
                CLAIMED_BY = NULL,
                CLAIMED_AT = NULL,
                LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT = NULL,
                COMPLETED_AT =
                CASE
                    WHEN RETRY_COUNT + 1 >= ?
                        THEN CURRENT TIMESTAMP
                    ELSE NULL
                END,
                UPDATED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                ERROR_CODE = 'STALE_CLAIM',
                ERROR_MESSAGE =
                    'Worker stopped before migration completed'
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND MIGRATION_STATUS = 'IN_PROGRESS'
              AND LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT <
                  CURRENT TIMESTAMP - %d MINUTES
            """.formatted(staleMinutes);

        return jdbcTemplate.update(
            sql,
            maximumRetries,
            maximumRetries,
            runId
        );
    }

    public void heartbeat(
            long runId,
            long customerId,
            String workerId) {

        jdbcTemplate.update(
            """
            UPDATE CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS
            SET LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                UPDATED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID = ?
              AND MIGRATION_STATUS = 'IN_PROGRESS'
              AND CLAIMED_BY = ?
            """,
            runId,
            customerId,
            workerId
        );
    }

    private static String truncate(
            String value,
            int maximumLength) {

        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }

        return value.substring(
            0,
            Math.min(value.length(), maximumLength)
        );
    }
}
```

Confirm the exact DB2 interval syntax used by your installed DB2 edition. Some environments may require constructing the stale cutoff timestamp in Java and binding it as a parameter.

# 16. Candidate customer repository

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.db2;

import java.util.List;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

@Repository
public class Db2CandidateRepository {

    private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;

    public Db2CandidateRepository(
            @Qualifier("db2JdbcTemplate")
            JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate) {

        this.jdbcTemplate = jdbcTemplate;
    }

    public List<Long> findUnclaimedCustomers(
            long runId,
            int limit) {

        return jdbcTemplate.queryForList(
            """
            SELECT c.CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
            FROM CUSTOMER_TO_BE_MIGRATED c
            WHERE c.RUN_ID = ?
              AND NOT EXISTS
              (
                  SELECT 1
                  FROM CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS s
                  WHERE s.RUN_ID = c.RUN_ID
                    AND s.CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID =
                        c.CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
              )
            ORDER BY
                c.PARTITION_ID,
                c.CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
            FETCH FIRST ? ROWS ONLY
            """,
            Long.class,
            runId,
            limit
        );
    }
}
```

Multiple workers may read overlapping candidates. The unique claim insert decides which worker owns each customer.

# 17. Customer claim service

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.service;

import com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.db2.Db2CandidateRepository;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.db2.Db2MigrationStatusRepository;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class CustomerClaimService {

    private final Db2CandidateRepository candidateRepository;
    private final Db2MigrationStatusRepository statusRepository;

    public List<Long> claimNextCustomers(
            long runId,
            String workerId,
            int candidateReadSize,
            int claimSize) {

        List<Long> candidates =
            candidateRepository.findUnclaimedCustomers(
                runId,
                candidateReadSize
            );

        List<Long> claimed = new ArrayList<>(claimSize);

        for (Long customerId : candidates) {
            boolean successfullyClaimed =
                statusRepository.claimCustomer(
                    runId,
                    customerId,
                    workerId
                );

            if (successfullyClaimed) {
                claimed.add(customerId);
            }

            if (claimed.size() >= claimSize) {
                break;
            }
        }

        return claimed;
    }
}
```

# 18. Read DB2 source snapshot rows

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.db2;

import com.company.personalisationmigration.model.CustomerPersonalisation;

import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

@Repository
public class Db2SourcePersonalisationRepository {

    private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;

    public Db2SourcePersonalisationRepository(
            @Qualifier("db2JdbcTemplate")
            JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate) {

        this.jdbcTemplate = jdbcTemplate;
    }

    public List<CustomerPersonalisation> findByCustomerIds(
            long runId,
            List<Long> customerIds) {

        if (customerIds.isEmpty()) {
            return List.of();
        }

        String placeholders = String.join(
            ",",
            Collections.nCopies(
                customerIds.size(),
                "?"
            )
        );

        String sql = """
            SELECT
                CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
                PERSONALISATION_TYPE,
                PERSONALISATION_KEY,
                PERSONALISATION_VALUE,
                SOURCE_CREATED_AT,
                SOURCE_UPDATED_AT
            FROM PERSONALISATION_TO_BE_MIGRATED
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
              AND CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID IN (%s)
            ORDER BY
                CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
                PERSONALISATION_TYPE,
                PERSONALISATION_KEY
            """.formatted(placeholders);

        Object[] parameters =
            new Object[customerIds.size() + 1];

        parameters[0] = runId;

        for (int i = 0; i < customerIds.size(); i++) {
            parameters[i + 1] = customerIds.get(i);
        }

        return jdbcTemplate.query(
            sql,
            (rs, rowNum) -> new CustomerPersonalisation(
                rs.getLong("CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID"),
                rs.getString("PERSONALISATION_TYPE"),
                rs.getString("PERSONALISATION_KEY"),
                rs.getString("PERSONALISATION_VALUE"),
                rs.getTimestamp("SOURCE_CREATED_AT") == null
                    ? null
                    : rs.getTimestamp(
                        "SOURCE_CREATED_AT"
                    ).toInstant(),
                rs.getTimestamp("SOURCE_UPDATED_AT") == null
                    ? null
                    : rs.getTimestamp(
                        "SOURCE_UPDATED_AT"
                    ).toInstant()
            ),
            parameters
        );
    }
}
```

# 19. MariaDB target repository

This method is customer-transactional: either all personalisation rows for one customer commit or all roll back.

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.mariadb;

import com.company.personalisationmigration.model.CustomerPersonalisation;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.model.MigrationOutcome;

import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Repository
public class MariaDbTargetRepository {

    private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;

    public MariaDbTargetRepository(
            @Qualifier("mariaDbJdbcTemplate")
            JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate) {

        this.jdbcTemplate = jdbcTemplate;
    }

    @Transactional(
        transactionManager = "mariaDbTransactionManager"
    )
    public MigrationOutcome upsertCustomer(
            long customerId,
            List<CustomerPersonalisation> rows) {

        AtomicInteger processed = new AtomicInteger();

        for (CustomerPersonalisation row : rows) {
            jdbcTemplate.update(
                """
                INSERT INTO TARGET_PERSONALISATION
                (
                    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
                    PERSONALISATION_TYPE,
                    PERSONALISATION_KEY,
                    PERSONALISATION_VALUE,
                    SOURCE_CREATED_AT,
                    SOURCE_UPDATED_AT
                )
                VALUES
                (
                    ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?
                )
                ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                    PERSONALISATION_VALUE =
                    CASE
                        WHEN VALUES(SOURCE_UPDATED_AT)
                             >= SOURCE_UPDATED_AT
                        THEN VALUES(PERSONALISATION_VALUE)
                        ELSE PERSONALISATION_VALUE
                    END,
                    SOURCE_UPDATED_AT =
                        GREATEST(
                            SOURCE_UPDATED_AT,
                            VALUES(SOURCE_UPDATED_AT)
                        )
                """,
                customerId,
                row.personalisationType(),
                row.personalisationKey(),
                row.personalisationValue(),
                toTimestamp(row.sourceCreatedAt()),
                toTimestamp(row.sourceUpdatedAt())
            );

            processed.incrementAndGet();
        }

        /*
         * MariaDB update counts for ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
         * can vary depending on driver settings.
         * For exact inserted-versus-updated counts, perform
         * an existence check before write or use business audit columns.
         */
        return new MigrationOutcome(
            processed.get(),
            0
        );
    }

    private static Timestamp toTimestamp(
            java.time.Instant value) {

        return value == null
            ? null
            : Timestamp.from(value);
    }
}
```

Ensure MariaDB has the actual business unique key:

```sql
ALTER TABLE TARGET_PERSONALISATION
ADD CONSTRAINT UK_TARGET_PERSONALISATION
UNIQUE
(
    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
    PERSONALISATION_TYPE,
    PERSONALISATION_KEY
);
```

# 20. Existing status-load service: 12:00 AM–2:00 AM

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.service;

import com.company.personalisationmigration.model.MigrationBatch;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.db2.Db2BatchControlRepository;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.db2.Db2MigrationStatusRepository;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.mariadb.MariaDbExistingCustomerRepository;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class ExistingStatusLoadService {

    private final Db2BatchControlRepository batchRepository;
    private final MariaDbExistingCustomerRepository mariaRepository;
    private final Db2MigrationStatusRepository statusRepository;

    public StatusLoadResult processNextBatch(
            long runId,
            String workerId,
            int pageSize) {

        Optional<MigrationBatch> optionalBatch =
            batchRepository.claimNextBatch(
                runId,
                "STATUS_LOAD",
                workerId
            );

        if (optionalBatch.isEmpty()) {
            return StatusLoadResult.noWork();
        }

        MigrationBatch batch = optionalBatch.get();

        long lastCustomerId =
            batch.lastProcessedId() != null
                ? batch.lastProcessedId()
                : batch.startCustomerId();

        long readCount = 0;
        long insertedCount = 0;

        try {
            batchRepository.markInProgress(
                batch,
                workerId
            );

            while (true) {
                List<Long> ids =
                    mariaRepository.findCustomerIds(
                        lastCustomerId,
                        batch.endCustomerId(),
                        pageSize
                    );

                if (ids.isEmpty()) {
                    break;
                }

                int inserted =
                    statusRepository.insertAlreadyMigrated(
                        runId,
                        ids
                    );

                readCount += ids.size();
                insertedCount += inserted;
                lastCustomerId = ids.get(ids.size() - 1);

                batchRepository.updateCheckpoint(
                    batch,
                    workerId,
                    lastCustomerId,
                    readCount,
                    insertedCount
                );

                if (batch.endCustomerId() != null
                        && lastCustomerId
                        >= batch.endCustomerId()) {
                    break;
                }
            }

            batchRepository.markCompleted(
                batch,
                workerId,
                readCount,
                insertedCount
            );

            return new StatusLoadResult(
                true,
                batch.batchId(),
                readCount,
                insertedCount
            );

        } catch (Exception exception) {
            batchRepository.markFailed(
                batch,
                workerId,
                exception.getMessage()
            );

            throw exception;
        }
    }

    public record StatusLoadResult(
        boolean workFound,
        long batchId,
        long readCount,
        long insertedCount
    ) {
        public static StatusLoadResult noWork() {
            return new StatusLoadResult(
                false,
                -1,
                0,
                0
            );
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 21. Bulk migration service: 2:00 AM–5:00 AM

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.service;

import com.company.personalisationmigration.config.MigrationProperties;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.model.BatchMigrationResult;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.model.CustomerPersonalisation;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.model.MigrationOutcome;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.db2.Db2MigrationStatusRepository;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.db2.Db2SourcePersonalisationRepository;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.mariadb.MariaDbExistingCustomerRepository;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.mariadb.MariaDbTargetRepository;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.function.Function;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.dao.TransientDataAccessException;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class BulkMigrationService {

    private final CustomerClaimService claimService;
    private final MariaDbExistingCustomerRepository existingRepository;
    private final Db2SourcePersonalisationRepository sourceRepository;
    private final MariaDbTargetRepository targetRepository;
    private final Db2MigrationStatusRepository statusRepository;
    private final MigrationProperties properties;

    public BatchMigrationResult processNextBatch(
            long runId,
            String workerId,
            int candidateReadSize,
            int claimSize) {

        List<Long> claimed =
            claimService.claimNextCustomers(
                runId,
                workerId,
                candidateReadSize,
                claimSize
            );

        return processClaimedCustomers(
            runId,
            workerId,
            claimed
        );
    }

    public BatchMigrationResult processClaimedCustomers(
            long runId,
            String workerId,
            List<Long> claimed) {

        if (claimed.isEmpty()) {
            return BatchMigrationResult.empty();
        }

        Set<Long> alreadyMigrated =
            existingRepository.findExistingCustomers(claimed);

        for (Long customerId : alreadyMigrated) {
            statusRepository.markAlreadyMigrated(
                runId,
                customerId,
                workerId
            );
        }

        List<Long> toMigrate = claimed.stream()
            .filter(id -> !alreadyMigrated.contains(id))
            .toList();

        List<CustomerPersonalisation> sourceRows =
            sourceRepository.findByCustomerIds(
                runId,
                toMigrate
            );

        Map<Long, List<CustomerPersonalisation>> rowsByCustomer =
            sourceRows.stream().collect(
                Collectors.groupingBy(
                    CustomerPersonalisation::customerSysGenId
                )
            );

        int migrated = 0;
        int retryPending = 0;
        int failed = 0;
        int sourceNotFound = 0;

        for (Long customerId : toMigrate) {
            List<CustomerPersonalisation> rows =
                rowsByCustomer.getOrDefault(
                    customerId,
                    List.of()
                );

            if (rows.isEmpty()) {
                statusRepository.markSourceNotFound(
                    runId,
                    customerId,
                    workerId
                );

                sourceNotFound++;
                continue;
            }

            try {
                statusRepository.heartbeat(
                    runId,
                    customerId,
                    workerId
                );

                MigrationOutcome outcome =
                    targetRepository.upsertCustomer(
                        customerId,
                        rows
                    );

                statusRepository.markMigrated(
                    runId,
                    customerId,
                    workerId,
                    rows.size(),
                    outcome.insertedCount(),
                    outcome.updatedCount()
                );

                migrated++;

            } catch (TransientDataAccessException exception) {
                statusRepository.markRetryPending(
                    runId,
                    customerId,
                    workerId,
                    properties.retry().maximumRetries(),
                    exception.getClass().getSimpleName(),
                    exception.getMessage()
                );

                retryPending++;

            } catch (Exception exception) {
                statusRepository.markFailed(
                    runId,
                    customerId,
                    workerId,
                    exception.getClass().getSimpleName(),
                    exception.getMessage()
                );

                failed++;
            }
        }

        return new BatchMigrationResult(
            claimed.size(),
            migrated,
            alreadyMigrated.size(),
            retryPending,
            failed,
            sourceNotFound
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 22. Existing status-load scheduler

All 4–6 PCF instances execute this scheduler. DB2 batch claiming prevents batch overlap.

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.scheduler;

import com.company.personalisationmigration.config.MigrationProperties;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.config.WorkerIdentity;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.service.ActiveRunService;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.service.ExistingStatusLoadService;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.service.MigrationWindowService;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;

import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class ExistingStatusLoadScheduler {

    private final MigrationWindowService windowService;
    private final ActiveRunService runService;
    private final ExistingStatusLoadService loadService;
    private final MigrationProperties properties;
    private final WorkerIdentity workerIdentity;

    @Scheduled(
        fixedDelayString =
            "${migration.status-load.scheduler-delay-ms:300000}"
    )
    public void execute() {

        if (!windowService.canLoadExistingStatuses()) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            var result = loadService.processNextBatch(
                runService.getActiveRunId(),
                workerIdentity.getWorkerId(),
                properties.statusLoad().pageSize()
            );

            log.info(
                "Status-load result: worker={}, batch={}, read={}, inserted={}",
                workerIdentity.getWorkerId(),
                result.batchId(),
                result.readCount(),
                result.insertedCount()
            );

        } catch (Exception exception) {
            log.error(
                "Status-load failed for worker {}",
                workerIdentity.getWorkerId(),
                exception
            );
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 23. Migration scheduler

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.scheduler;

import com.company.personalisationmigration.config.MigrationProperties;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.config.WorkerIdentity;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.service.ActiveRunService;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.service.BulkMigrationService;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.service.MigrationWindowService;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;

import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class MigrationExecutionScheduler {

    private final MigrationWindowService windowService;
    private final ActiveRunService runService;
    private final WorkerIdentity workerIdentity;
    private final MigrationProperties properties;
    private final BulkMigrationService migrationService;

    @Scheduled(
        fixedDelayString =
            "${migration.execution.scheduler-delay-ms:3000}"
    )
    public void execute() {

        if (!windowService.canClaimMigrationWork()) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            var result = migrationService.processNextBatch(
                runService.getActiveRunId(),
                workerIdentity.getWorkerId(),
                properties.execution().candidateReadSize(),
                properties.execution().claimSize()
            );

            if (result.claimedCount() > 0) {
                log.info(
                    """
                    Migration batch completed:
                    worker={}, claimed={}, migrated={},
                    alreadyMigrated={}, retryPending={},
                    failed={}, sourceNotFound={}
                    """,
                    workerIdentity.getWorkerId(),
                    result.claimedCount(),
                    result.migratedCount(),
                    result.alreadyMigratedCount(),
                    result.retryPendingCount(),
                    result.failedCount(),
                    result.sourceNotFoundCount()
                );
            }

        } catch (Exception exception) {
            log.error(
                "Migration scheduler failed for worker {}",
                workerIdentity.getWorkerId(),
                exception
            );
        }
    }
}
```

# 24. Retry service and scheduler

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.service;

import com.company.personalisationmigration.config.MigrationProperties;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.db2.Db2MigrationStatusRepository;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class RetryService {

    private final Db2MigrationStatusRepository statusRepository;
    private final BulkMigrationService bulkMigrationService;
    private final MigrationProperties properties;

    public int processRetryBatch(
            long runId,
            String workerId) {

        List<Long> candidates =
            statusRepository.findRetryCandidates(
                runId,
                properties.retry().maximumRetries(),
                properties.retry().claimSize() * 3
            );

        List<Long> claimed = new ArrayList<>();

        for (Long customerId : candidates) {
            boolean success =
                statusRepository.claimRetry(
                    runId,
                    customerId,
                    workerId,
                    properties.retry().maximumRetries()
                );

            if (success) {
                claimed.add(customerId);
            }

            if (claimed.size()
                    >= properties.retry().claimSize()) {
                break;
            }
        }

        bulkMigrationService.processClaimedCustomers(
            runId,
            workerId,
            claimed
        );

        return claimed.size();
    }
}
```

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.scheduler;

import com.company.personalisationmigration.config.WorkerIdentity;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.service.ActiveRunService;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.service.MigrationWindowService;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.service.RetryService;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;

import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class RetryScheduler {

    private final MigrationWindowService windowService;
    private final ActiveRunService runService;
    private final WorkerIdentity workerIdentity;
    private final RetryService retryService;

    @Scheduled(
        fixedDelayString =
            "${migration.retry.scheduler-delay-ms:30000}"
    )
    public void execute() {

        if (!windowService.canRetry()) {
            return;
        }

        int claimed = retryService.processRetryBatch(
            runService.getActiveRunId(),
            workerIdentity.getWorkerId()
        );

        if (claimed > 0) {
            log.info(
                "Retry worker {} claimed {} customers",
                workerIdentity.getWorkerId(),
                claimed
            );
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 25. Stale claim recovery

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.scheduler;

import com.company.personalisationmigration.config.MigrationProperties;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.repository.db2.Db2MigrationStatusRepository;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.service.ActiveRunService;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;

import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class StaleClaimRecoveryScheduler {

    private final ActiveRunService runService;
    private final MigrationProperties properties;
    private final Db2MigrationStatusRepository statusRepository;

    @Scheduled(
        fixedDelayString =
            "${migration.recovery.scheduler-delay-ms:600000}"
    )
    public void execute() {

        int recovered = statusRepository.recoverStaleClaims(
            runService.getActiveRunId(),
            properties.retry().maximumRetries(),
            properties.recovery().staleClaimMinutes()
        );

        if (recovered > 0) {
            log.warn(
                "Recovered {} stale customer claims",
                recovered
            );
        }
    }
}
```

All instances may execute recovery. The conditional SQL makes repeated recovery safe, although optionally only one operational instance can run it.

## Controller

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.controller;

import com.company.personalisationmigration.config.MigrationProperties;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.config.WorkerIdentity;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.controller.dto.ManualMigrationRequest;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.controller.dto.ManualMigrationResponse;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.controller.dto.ManualStatusLoadRequest;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.model.BatchMigrationResult;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.service.BulkMigrationService;
import com.company.personalisationmigration.service.ExistingStatusLoadService;

import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;

import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/internal/migrations")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class MigrationController {

    private final BulkMigrationService migrationService;
    private final ExistingStatusLoadService statusLoadService;
    private final WorkerIdentity workerIdentity;
    private final MigrationProperties properties;

    @PostMapping("/{runId}/load-existing")
    public ResponseEntity<?> loadExisting(
            @PathVariable long runId,
            @Valid @RequestBody
            ManualStatusLoadRequest request) {

        long read = 0;
        long inserted = 0;
        int executed = 0;

        for (int i = 0;
             i < request.maximumBatches();
             i++) {

            var result = statusLoadService.processNextBatch(
                runId,
                workerIdentity.getWorkerId(),
                request.pageSize()
            );

            if (!result.workFound()) {
                break;
            }

            executed++;
            read += result.readCount();
            inserted += result.insertedCount();
        }

        return ResponseEntity.ok(
            new ManualStatusLoadResponse(
                executed,
                read,
                inserted
            )
        );
    }

    @PostMapping("/{runId}/migrate")
    public ResponseEntity<ManualMigrationResponse> migrate(
            @PathVariable long runId,
            @Valid @RequestBody
            ManualMigrationRequest request) {

        int batchesExecuted = 0;
        int totalClaimed = 0;
        int migrated = 0;
        int alreadyMigrated = 0;
        int retryPending = 0;
        int failed = 0;
        int sourceNotFound = 0;

        for (int i = 0;
             i < request.maximumBatches();
             i++) {

            BatchMigrationResult result =
                migrationService.processNextBatch(
                    runId,
                    workerIdentity.getWorkerId(),
                    Math.max(
                        request.claimSize() * 4,
                        properties.execution()
                            .candidateReadSize()
                    ),
                    request.claimSize()
                );

            if (result.claimedCount() == 0) {
                break;
            }

            batchesExecuted++;
            totalClaimed += result.claimedCount();
            migrated += result.migratedCount();
            alreadyMigrated +=
                result.alreadyMigratedCount();
            retryPending +=
                result.retryPendingCount();
            failed += result.failedCount();
            sourceNotFound +=
                result.sourceNotFoundCount();
        }

        return ResponseEntity.ok(
            new ManualMigrationResponse(
                batchesExecuted,
                totalClaimed,
                migrated,
                alreadyMigrated,
                retryPending,
                failed,
                sourceNotFound
            )
        );
    }

    public record ManualStatusLoadResponse(
        int batchesExecuted,
        long customersRead,
        long statusesInserted
    ) {}
}
```

Secure these endpoints with your organisation’s standard authentication and a migration-operator permission.

# 27. Reconciliation repository/service

```java
package com.company.personalisationmigration.service;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
public class ReconciliationService {

    private final JdbcTemplate db2JdbcTemplate;

    public ReconciliationService(
            @Qualifier("db2JdbcTemplate")
            JdbcTemplate db2JdbcTemplate) {

        this.db2JdbcTemplate = db2JdbcTemplate;
    }

    public ReconciliationSummary summarize(long runId) {

        long eligibleCustomers =
            db2JdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                """
                SELECT COUNT(*)
                FROM CUSTOMER_TO_BE_MIGRATED
                WHERE RUN_ID = ?
                """,
                Long.class,
                runId
            );

        long withoutStatus =
            db2JdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                """
                SELECT COUNT(*)
                FROM CUSTOMER_TO_BE_MIGRATED c
                WHERE c.RUN_ID = ?
                  AND NOT EXISTS
                  (
                      SELECT 1
                      FROM CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS s
                      WHERE s.RUN_ID = c.RUN_ID
                        AND s.CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID =
                            c.CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
                  )
                """,
                Long.class,
                runId
            );

        Map<String, Long> statusCounts =
            new LinkedHashMap<>();

        db2JdbcTemplate.query(
            """
            SELECT
                MIGRATION_STATUS,
                COUNT(*) AS CUSTOMER_COUNT
            FROM CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_STATUS
            WHERE RUN_ID = ?
            GROUP BY MIGRATION_STATUS
            ORDER BY MIGRATION_STATUS
            """,
            rs -> statusCounts.put(
                rs.getString("MIGRATION_STATUS"),
                rs.getLong("CUSTOMER_COUNT")
            ),
            runId
        );

        return new ReconciliationSummary(
            runId,
            eligibleCustomers,
            withoutStatus,
            statusCounts
        );
    }

    public record ReconciliationSummary(
        long runId,
        long eligibleCustomers,
        long eligibleWithoutStatus,
        Map<String, Long> statusCounts
    ) {}
}
```

Add endpoint:

```java
@GetMapping("/{runId}/status")
public ResponseEntity<?> status(
        @PathVariable long runId) {

    return ResponseEntity.ok(
        reconciliationService.summarize(runId)
    );
}
```

# 28. Creating status-load ranges

Instead of offsets, create ranges in advance.

Example:

```sql
INSERT INTO MIGRATION_BATCH_CONTROL
(
    RUN_ID,
    PHASE,
    BATCH_ID,
    START_CUSTOMER_ID,
    END_CUSTOMER_ID,
    LAST_PROCESSED_ID,
    BATCH_STATUS
)
VALUES
(20260731, 'STATUS_LOAD', 1, 0,       500000,  0,       'PENDING'),
(20260731, 'STATUS_LOAD', 2, 500000,  1000000, 500000,  'PENDING'),
(20260731, 'STATUS_LOAD', 3, 1000000, 1500000, 1000000, 'PENDING');
```

Create enough ranges so that there are significantly more ranges than workers.

For six workers:

```text
30–60 status-load ranges
```

Each range may logically cover 100,000 or 500,000 customer IDs, but it is read internally in pages of 10,000.

---

# 29. Recommended initial production configuration

For three instances in each DC:

```text
DCE: 3
DCW: 3
Total workers: 6
```

Start with:

```yaml
migration:
  status-load:
    page-size: 10000
    scheduler-delay-ms: 300000

  execution:
    candidate-read-size: 500
    claim-size: 100
    scheduler-delay-ms: 3000

  retry:
    claim-size: 50
    maximum-retries: 3

  recovery:
    stale-claim-minutes: 15
```

Initially, process customers sequentially inside each instance.

Expected concurrency:

```text
6 instances
× 1 active migration thread
= 6 concurrent customer migrations
```

If throughput is insufficient and the databases remain healthy, introduce a bounded executor with two threads per instance:

```text
6 instances × 2 threads
= 12 concurrent customer migrations
```

Do not use an unbounded executor or `parallelStream()`.

---

# 30. Important implementation behaviour

## Scheduler and endpoint run together

Both use:

```text
Read candidates
    ↓
Insert DB2 IN_PROGRESS claim
    ↓
Only successful claim proceeds
```

Therefore, a manual endpoint call does not deliberately duplicate scheduler work.

## MariaDB succeeds but DB2 status update fails

The customer remains `IN_PROGRESS`.

Later:

```text
Stale recovery
    ↓
RETRY_PENDING
    ↓
Retry
    ↓
MariaDB existence check finds customer
    ↓
ALREADY_MIGRATED
```

## Customer seamlessly migrates after 2:00 AM

The bulk process:

1. Claims customer in DB2.
2. Rechecks MariaDB.
3. Marks `ALREADY_MIGRATED` when found.

If the seamless migration occurs after the check but before the insert, MariaDB’s unique constraint and upsert prevent duplicate target rows.

## One DC or instance fails

No permanent customer ranges are assigned to a DC or `CF_INSTANCE_INDEX`.

Healthy workers continue claiming remaining customers.

---

# 31. Recommended enhancements before production

Add these after the basic implementation works:

* Micrometer counters for claimed, migrated, failed and retried customers
* Timer for customer migration duration
* Circuit breaker when DB2 or MariaDB is unhealthy
* Configurable pause flag in `MIGRATION_RUN`
* Maximum batch execution duration
* Target row-count validation
* Structured audit logging with `RUN_ID`, `CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID` and `WORKER_ID`
* Integration tests using a DB2-compatible test environment and MariaDB Testcontainers
* A pilot run of at least 10,000–25,000 customers

The most important methods to production-test are:

```text
Db2MigrationStatusRepository.claimCustomer()
MariaDbTargetRepository.upsertCustomer()
Db2MigrationStatusRepository.recoverStaleClaims()
ExistingStatusLoadService.processNextBatch()
BulkMigrationService.processNextBatch()
```


How about export the data of personalisation for last 6 months logged in users Add runid column, status column add index too Scheduler every x mins Created a runid , update the staging personalisation db with run id for first x rows where runid is null Read the data for the same run id Loop through and read data from maria db for the cust sys gen id, if exists update status and already migrated otherwise insert script to MariaDB, update the status and migrated If fails - update as failure How it works ? How to optimize Pros and cons


Yes, this design can work. It is essentially a **row-claiming staging-table migration pattern**.

The main question is whether the `run_id` is being used as:

1. A migration execution identifier, or
2. A temporary worker claim identifier.

Those are different concerns. For reliability, keep both:

```text
MIGRATION_RUN_ID  → identifies the overall migration run
CLAIM_BATCH_ID    → identifies one scheduler batch claimed by one worker
```

Using only `RUN_ID` to claim the next rows can become confusing when several application instances are running.

# Proposed flow

```text
DB2 BAU tables
    CUSTOMER
    PERSONALISATION
          │
          │ last login within 6 months
          │ soft_delete = 0
          ▼
PERSONALISATION_MIGRATION_STAGE
          │
          ├── MIGRATION_RUN_ID
          ├── CLAIM_BATCH_ID
          ├── MIGRATION_STATUS
          ├── CLAIMED_BY
          ├── CLAIMED_AT
          ├── RETRY_COUNT
          └── ERROR details
          │
          ▼
2–6 Spring Boot workers
          │
          ├── atomically claim rows
          ├── group by customer
          ├── check MariaDB
          ├── migrate or mark existing
          └── update final status
          ▼
MariaDB
```

# 1. Staging table design

Do not add only `RUN_ID` and `STATUS`. Add enough information for distributed claiming and restart recovery.

```sql
CREATE TABLE PERSONALISATION_MIGRATION_STAGE
(
    STAGE_ID                  BIGINT NOT NULL
                              GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,

    MIGRATION_RUN_ID          BIGINT NOT NULL,

    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID       BIGINT NOT NULL,

    PERSONALISATION_TYPE      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    PERSONALISATION_KEY       VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    PERSONALISATION_VALUE     VARCHAR(4000),

    SOURCE_CREATED_AT         TIMESTAMP,
    SOURCE_UPDATED_AT         TIMESTAMP,

    MIGRATION_STATUS          VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL
                              DEFAULT 'PENDING',

    CLAIM_BATCH_ID            VARCHAR(100),
    CLAIMED_BY                VARCHAR(200),
    CLAIMED_AT                TIMESTAMP,
    LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT         TIMESTAMP,

    RETRY_COUNT               INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    COMPLETED_AT              TIMESTAMP,

    ERROR_CODE                VARCHAR(100),
    ERROR_MESSAGE             VARCHAR(2000),

    CREATED_AT                TIMESTAMP NOT NULL
                              DEFAULT CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
    UPDATED_AT                TIMESTAMP NOT NULL
                              DEFAULT CURRENT TIMESTAMP,

    PRIMARY KEY (STAGE_ID)
);
```

Recommended statuses:

```text
PENDING
IN_PROGRESS
MIGRATED
ALREADY_MIGRATED
RETRY_PENDING
FAILED
```

# 2. Required indexes

For claiming:

```sql
CREATE INDEX IDX_STAGE_CLAIM
ON PERSONALISATION_MIGRATION_STAGE
(
    MIGRATION_RUN_ID,
    MIGRATION_STATUS,
    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
    STAGE_ID
);
```

For retrieving claimed rows:

```sql
CREATE INDEX IDX_STAGE_BATCH
ON PERSONALISATION_MIGRATION_STAGE
(
    MIGRATION_RUN_ID,
    CLAIM_BATCH_ID,
    MIGRATION_STATUS
);
```

For customer-level processing:

```sql
CREATE INDEX IDX_STAGE_CUSTOMER
ON PERSONALISATION_MIGRATION_STAGE
(
    MIGRATION_RUN_ID,
    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
    MIGRATION_STATUS
);
```

For stale recovery:

```sql
CREATE INDEX IDX_STAGE_STALE
ON PERSONALISATION_MIGRATION_STAGE
(
    MIGRATION_RUN_ID,
    MIGRATION_STATUS,
    LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT
);
```

The claim index is the most important.

# 3. Exporting the six-month data

Use a fixed cutoff timestamp for the run.

Example:

```text
Eligibility start: 2026-01-31 00:00:00
Eligibility cutoff: 2026-07-31 00:00:00
```

Do not use `CURRENT TIMESTAMP - 6 MONTHS` repeatedly after the run starts. Otherwise, the eligible population moves during migration.

Populate staging:

```sql
INSERT INTO PERSONALISATION_MIGRATION_STAGE
(
    MIGRATION_RUN_ID,
    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
    PERSONALISATION_TYPE,
    PERSONALISATION_KEY,
    PERSONALISATION_VALUE,
    SOURCE_CREATED_AT,
    SOURCE_UPDATED_AT,
    MIGRATION_STATUS
)
SELECT
    :RUN_ID,
    p.CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
    p.PERSONALISATION_TYPE,
    p.PERSONALISATION_KEY,
    p.PERSONALISATION_VALUE,
    p.CREATED_AT,
    p.UPDATED_AT,
    'PENDING'
FROM PERSONALISATION p
INNER JOIN CUSTOMER c
    ON c.CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID =
       p.CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
WHERE p.SOFT_DELETE = 0
  AND c.LAST_LOGON_DATETIME >= :START_TIME
  AND c.LAST_LOGON_DATETIME < :CUTOFF_TIME;
```

Prevent duplicate staging rows for the same run.

Use the source business key:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX UK_STAGE_SOURCE_ROW
ON PERSONALISATION_MIGRATION_STAGE
(
    MIGRATION_RUN_ID,
    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
    PERSONALISATION_TYPE,
    PERSONALISATION_KEY
);
```

This makes population restart-safe.

# 4. Important issue with claiming the first X rows

Your proposed logic is:

```text
Create run ID
Update first X rows where run_id is null
Read rows for that run ID
Process them
```

This has two problems.

## Problem 1: one run ID should represent the whole migration

If each scheduler execution creates a new run ID, reporting becomes fragmented:

```text
Run 101 → 1,000 rows
Run 102 → 1,000 rows
Run 103 → 1,000 rows
```

It becomes harder to answer:

```text
How many of the six-month population were migrated?
```

Better:

```text
One MIGRATION_RUN_ID for the complete migration
One CLAIM_BATCH_ID for each scheduler execution
```

## Problem 2: rows belonging to the same customer may be split

Suppose customer 123 has 20 personalisation rows.

The first-X-row update can claim:

```text
Batch A → 12 rows for customer 123
Batch B → remaining 8 rows
```

Two instances can then process the same customer at once.

That is dangerous if the migration logic works customer-by-customer.

You should claim by **customer**, not arbitrary staging rows.

# 5. Best optimized structure

Keep the 3.8 million or larger personalisation staging table, but also create a small distinct-customer work table.

```sql
CREATE TABLE CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_WORK
(
    MIGRATION_RUN_ID       BIGINT NOT NULL,
    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID    BIGINT NOT NULL,

    MIGRATION_STATUS       VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL
                           DEFAULT 'PENDING',

    CLAIM_BATCH_ID         VARCHAR(100),
    CLAIMED_BY             VARCHAR(200),
    CLAIMED_AT             TIMESTAMP,
    LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT      TIMESTAMP,

    RETRY_COUNT            INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    SOURCE_ROW_COUNT       INTEGER,
    TARGET_ROW_COUNT       INTEGER,

    COMPLETED_AT           TIMESTAMP,

    ERROR_CODE             VARCHAR(100),
    ERROR_MESSAGE          VARCHAR(2000),

    CREATED_AT             TIMESTAMP NOT NULL
                           DEFAULT CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
    UPDATED_AT             TIMESTAMP NOT NULL
                           DEFAULT CURRENT TIMESTAMP,

    PRIMARY KEY
    (
        MIGRATION_RUN_ID,
        CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
    )
);
```

Populate it once:

```sql
INSERT INTO CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_WORK
(
    MIGRATION_RUN_ID,
    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
    MIGRATION_STATUS,
    SOURCE_ROW_COUNT
)
SELECT
    MIGRATION_RUN_ID,
    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
    'PENDING',
    COUNT(*)
FROM PERSONALISATION_MIGRATION_STAGE
WHERE MIGRATION_RUN_ID = :RUN_ID
GROUP BY
    MIGRATION_RUN_ID,
    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID;
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE INDEX IDX_CMW_CLAIM
ON CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_WORK
(
    MIGRATION_RUN_ID,
    MIGRATION_STATUS,
    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
);
```

```sql
CREATE INDEX IDX_CMW_BATCH
ON CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_WORK
(
    MIGRATION_RUN_ID,
    CLAIM_BATCH_ID,
    MIGRATION_STATUS
);
```

Now claim customers, not personalisation rows.

# 6. Atomic claiming

Every scheduler execution creates a batch ID:

```java
String claimBatchId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
```

Worker ID:

```text
DCE-0-guid
DCW-2-guid
```

Claim X customers:

```sql
UPDATE CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_WORK
SET MIGRATION_STATUS = 'IN_PROGRESS',
    CLAIM_BATCH_ID = ?,
    CLAIMED_BY = ?,
    CLAIMED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
    LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
    UPDATED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP
WHERE MIGRATION_RUN_ID = ?
  AND MIGRATION_STATUS IN
      ('PENDING', 'RETRY_PENDING')
ORDER BY CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
FETCH FIRST ? ROWS ONLY;
```

Depending on the DB2 version, `ORDER BY ... FETCH FIRST` may not be valid directly in an update. A safer DB2 pattern is to select the IDs first and then conditionally update them.

## Claim selected customer IDs

Select:

```sql
SELECT CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
FROM CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_WORK
WHERE MIGRATION_RUN_ID = ?
  AND MIGRATION_STATUS = 'PENDING'
ORDER BY CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
FETCH FIRST ? ROWS ONLY;
```

Then for each selected customer:

```sql
UPDATE CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_WORK
SET MIGRATION_STATUS = 'IN_PROGRESS',
    CLAIM_BATCH_ID = ?,
    CLAIMED_BY = ?,
    CLAIMED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
    LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
    UPDATED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP
WHERE MIGRATION_RUN_ID = ?
  AND CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID = ?
  AND MIGRATION_STATUS = 'PENDING';
```

Only keep customers where the update count is `1`.

That condition prevents two workers from claiming the same customer.

# 7. Claim service code

```java
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class CustomerWorkClaimService {

    private final JdbcTemplate db2JdbcTemplate;

    public ClaimedBatch claimNextBatch(
            long runId,
            String workerId,
            int claimSize) {

        String claimBatchId =
                UUID.randomUUID().toString();

        List<Long> candidates =
                db2JdbcTemplate.queryForList(
                    """
                    SELECT CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
                    FROM CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_WORK
                    WHERE MIGRATION_RUN_ID = ?
                      AND MIGRATION_STATUS IN
                          ('PENDING', 'RETRY_PENDING')
                    ORDER BY CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
                    FETCH FIRST ? ROWS ONLY
                    """,
                    Long.class,
                    runId,
                    claimSize * 3
                );

        List<Long> claimed = new ArrayList<>();

        for (Long customerId : candidates) {
            int updated = db2JdbcTemplate.update(
                """
                UPDATE CUSTOMER_MIGRATION_WORK
                SET MIGRATION_STATUS = 'IN_PROGRESS',
                    CLAIM_BATCH_ID = ?,
                    CLAIMED_BY = ?,
                    CLAIMED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                    LAST_HEARTBEAT_AT =
                        CURRENT TIMESTAMP,
                    UPDATED_AT = CURRENT TIMESTAMP
                WHERE MIGRATION_RUN_ID = ?
                  AND CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID = ?
                  AND MIGRATION_STATUS IN
                      ('PENDING', 'RETRY_PENDING')
                """,
                claimBatchId,
                workerId,
                runId,
                customerId
            );

            if (updated == 1) {
                claimed.add(customerId);
            }

            if (claimed.size() >= claimSize) {
                break;
            }
        }

        return new ClaimedBatch(
            claimBatchId,
            claimed
        );
    }
}
```

# 8. Read all personalisation rows for claimed customers

```sql
SELECT
    STAGE_ID,
    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
    PERSONALISATION_TYPE,
    PERSONALISATION_KEY,
    PERSONALISATION_VALUE,
    SOURCE_CREATED_AT,
    SOURCE_UPDATED_AT
FROM PERSONALISATION_MIGRATION_STAGE
WHERE MIGRATION_RUN_ID = ?
  AND CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID IN
      (?, ?, ?, ...)
ORDER BY
    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
    STAGE_ID;
```

Keep claim size limited because the personalisation row count can be much larger than the customer count.

Recommended initial claim size:

```text
50–100 customers per worker
```

If each customer averages 12 rows:

```text
100 customers ≈ 1,200 staging rows
```

That is manageable.

# 9. Check MariaDB efficiently

Do not query MariaDB separately once per customer.

Avoid:

```text
Customer 1 → query
Customer 2 → query
Customer 3 → query
...
```

Use one batch query:

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
FROM TARGET_PERSONALISATION
WHERE CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID IN
(
    ?, ?, ?, ...
);
```

Then split:

```text
Existing customers
    → ALREADY_MIGRATED

Missing customers
    → insert/upsert
```

Java:

```java
Set<Long> existingCustomerIds =
        mariaDbRepository.findExistingCustomers(
            claimedCustomerIds
        );

List<Long> missingCustomerIds =
        claimedCustomerIds.stream()
            .filter(id ->
                !existingCustomerIds.contains(id))
            .toList();
```

# 10. Should an existing customer mean fully migrated?

This is an important risk.

Checking only:

```text
Does customer ID exist in MariaDB?
```

may be insufficient.

Example:

```text
DB2 staging contains 15 personalisation rows
MariaDB contains only 4 rows
```

The customer exists, but migration is incomplete.

Therefore, determine what “already migrated” means.

## Weak check

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID
FROM TARGET_PERSONALISATION
WHERE CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID IN (...);
```

Fast, but may mark partially migrated customers as complete.

## Better check

Compare business keys or row counts.

```sql
SELECT
    CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID,
    COUNT(*) AS TARGET_COUNT
FROM TARGET_PERSONALISATION
WHERE CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID IN (...)
GROUP BY CUSTOMER_SYS_GEN_ID;
```
